# A55 — iOS request-building and write guarantees against Android

Read-only static audit, 2026-10-15. **Android keeps the ordinary search request fields and most of the checked collection/challenge write fences.** The highest-impact gaps are a private bookmark prefilled as public, comment duplicate protection that does not survive reopening, hidden posting pseuds being dropped, and an Unmark button that changes only the local list.

Android evidence is pinned exclusively to **`f4a7a2838d381987aed7c2b20f3796fbea4aec6b`**, read with `git show`/`git grep` in `/Users/cidy02/kudos-android-lane`. Its checked-out HEAD was `0d1d9566f6b615980bf034db09e4d63231c47f43`; current Android files were not substituted. iOS evidence is the tracked tests/code in `/Users/cidy02/kudos-ios-polish`, HEAD **`76473fc9571798070c0d11ed7cbdc157ead222a9`**, with no tracked modifications reported. Existing untracked Packages/Vendor in iOS and docs/android-port/digest-input in Android were left alone. `KudosTests/…` paths below resolve in that iOS worktree; `android/…` paths resolve at the pinned Android revision. Ranges are inclusive; separate quoted expressions retain their individual source ranges and have whitespace collapsed for table layout.

Method follows A52/A54: compare the reader guarantee with the actual Kotlin path, not helper names or the presence of an Android test. All `@Test` functions in all seventeen requested files are included, including the second suite in DownloadOnSubscribeTests.swift. Inline HTML/model inputs are assessed as supplied; production integration differences and representation differences are identified explicitly. **Kept is a static implementation judgment, not a claim that Kotlin ran the Swift test.** Exact HTTP-pair differences are marked missing even when browser/Rails scalar semantics give the right final value. Missing tests alone never establish a missing rule. Unsure means the comparison remains unproved; not applicable names an absent platform/API feature. Named Android tests are related evidence, with narrower coverage called out; none was executed.

Binding reference: `docs/AO3_NETWORKING_POLICY.md`: explicit reader-requested reads, writes only on a tap, sent once and never retried, session-bound preparation/dispatch, and positive AO3 evidence before reporting success. This is an audit of the listed tests and their connected paths, not a blanket certification of every write in the app.

Coverage: **213 tests: 158 kept, 45 missing, 6 unsure, 4 not applicable.** Many missing rows describe the same absent comment-guard machinery; these are not 45 independent bugs.

{ if (rating == AO3Rating.ANY) { return if (includeNotRated) null else "-rating_ids:${AO3Rating.NOT_RATED.ao3Id}" } val ratings = selectedRatings() if (ratings.size &lt;= 1) return null return ratings.joinToString( separator = " OR ", prefix = "(", postfix = ")" ) { "rating_ids:${it.ao3Id}" } } private fun selectedRatings(): List&lt;AO3Rating&gt; { if (rating == AO3Rating.ANY) return emptyList() if (rating == AO3Rating.NOT_RATED) return listOf(AO3Rating.NOT_RATED) val ranked = listOf( AO3Rating.GENERAL, AO3Rating.TEEN, AO3Rating.MATURE, AO3Rating.EXPLICIT ) val index = ranked.indexOf(rating) if (index &lt; 0) return emptyList() val selected = when (ratingMatch) { AO3RatingMatch.EXACT -&gt; ranked.subList(index, index + 1) AO3RatingMatch.OR_HIGHER -&gt; ranked.subList(index, ranked.size) AO3RatingMatch.OR_LOWER -&gt; ranked.subList(0, index + 1) }.toMutableList() if (includeNotRated) selected += AO3Rating.NOT_RATED return selected</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/search/AO3SearchUrlBuilder.kt:76–87` <code>add("work_search[query]", filters.searchQuery) add("work_search[title]", filters.title) add("work_search[creators]", filters.creators) add("work_search[fandom_names]", filters.fandom) add("work_search[character_names]", filters.characters) add("work_search[relationship_names]", filters.relationships) add("work_search[freeform_names]", filters.additionalTags) // AO3's own exclusion field. | Test — guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |

| `aFandomBecomesAO3sOwnTagPath`<br>A fandom becomes AO3's escaped tag path. | `KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:26` <code>#expect(url.absoluteString.hasPrefix("https://archiveofourown.org/tags/Naruto%20(Anime%20*a*%20Manga)/works?"))</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/search/AO3SearchUrlBuilder.kt:32–40` <code>fun buildFandomWorksUrl(fandom: String, filters: AO3SearchFilters, page: Int = 1): String? { val segment = tagPathSegment(fandom) ?: return null return AO3Constants.baseHttpUrl.newBuilder() .addPathSegment("tags") .addPathSegment(segment) .addPathSegment("works") .also { appendWorkSearchParams(it, filters, page) } .build() .toString()</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/search/AO3SearchUrlBuilder.kt:49–56` <code>fun tagPathSegment(tag: String): String? { val trimmed = tag.trim() if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return null var escaped = trimmed for ((from, to) in listOf("/" to "*s*", "&amp;" to "*a*", "." to "*d*", "?" to "*q*", "#" to "*h*")) { escaped = escaped.replace(from, to) } return escaped</code> | OkHttp percent-encodes spaces after the five AO3 substitutions. Android test: AO3FandomWorksUrlTest.aFandomBecomesAO3sOwnTagPath |
| `everyCharacterAO3EscapesIsEscaped`<br>Slash, ampersand, dot, question, hash and spaces receive the correct path encoding. | `KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:30` <code>#expect(AO3Client.tagPathSegment("a/b") == "a*s*b")</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:31` <code>#expect(AO3Client.tagPathSegment("a&amp;b") == "a*a*b")</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:32` <code>#expect(AO3Client.tagPathSegment("a.b") == "a*d*b")</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:33` <code>#expect(AO3Client.tagPathSegment("a?b") == "a*q*b")</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:34` <code>#expect(AO3Client.tagPathSegment("a#b") == "a*h*b")</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:36` <code>#expect(AO3Client.tagPathSegment("a b") == "a%20b")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/search/AO3SearchUrlBuilder.kt:36` <code>.addPathSegment(segment)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/search/AO3SearchUrlBuilder.kt:49–56` <code>fun tagPathSegment(tag: String): String? { val trimmed = tag.trim() if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return null var escaped = trimmed for ((from, to) in listOf("/" to "*s*", "&amp;" to "*a*", "." to "*d*", "?" to "*q*", "#" to "*h*")) { escaped = escaped.replace(from, to) } return escaped</code> | The helper returns raw spaces, but addPathSegment encodes them on the actual request; helper representation differs without a wrong URL. Android test: AO3FandomWorksUrlTest.everyCharacterAO3EscapesIsEscaped |
| `browseSendsTheSameFiltersAsSearch`<br>Browse transmits every listed search filter, including exclusions and numeric bounds. | `KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:58` <code>#expect(values["work_search[title]"] == ["Chunin Exams"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:59` <code>#expect(values["work_search[creators]"] == ["someauthor"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:60` <code>#expect(values["work_search[character_names]"] == ["Sasuke Uchiha"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:61` <code>#expect(values["work_search[rating_ids]"] == ["11"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:62` <code>#expect(values["work_search[single_chapter]"] == ["1"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:63` <code>#expect(values["work_search[revised_at]"] == ["&lt; 1 week ago"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:64` <code>#expect(values["work_search[hits]"] == ["&gt; 1000"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:65` <code>#expect(values["work_search[word_count]"] == ["1000-5000"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:66` <code>#expect(values["work_search[excluded_tag_names]"] == ["Time Travel"])</code><br>`KudosTests/FandomWorksURLTests.swift:67` <code>#expect(values["work_search[sort_column]"] == ["kudos_count"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/search/AO3SearchUrlBuilder.kt:76–114` <code>add("work_search[query]", filters.searchQuery) add("work_search[title]", filters.title) add("work_search[creators]", filters.creators) add("work_search[fandom_names]", filters.fandom) add("work_search[character_names]", filters.characters) add("work_search[relationship_names]", filters.relationships) add("work_search[freeform_names]", filters.additionalTags) // AO3's own exclusion field. Not on the search *form*, but it is in // `WorkSearchForm::ATTRIBUTES` and the endpoint honours it — see // AO3SearchFilters.excludedTagNames for why this beats `-"tag"` in the query. add("work_search[excluded_tag_names]", filters.excludedTagNames) add("work_search[rating_ids]", filters.structuredRatingId) AO3Warning.entries .filter(filters.warnings::contains) .forEach { builder.addQueryParameter("work_search[archive_warning_ids][]", it.ao3Id) } AO3Category.entries .filter(filters.categories::contains) .forEach { builder.addQueryParameter("work_search[category_ids][]", it.ao3Id) } add("work_search[crossover]", filters.crossover.ao3Value) add("work_search[complete]", filters.completion.ao3Value) add("work_search[single_chapter]", filters.chapterCount.ao3Value) add("work_search[word_count]", wordCountExpression(filters)) add("work_search[hits]", rangeExpression(filters.hitsFrom, filters.hitsTo)) add("work_search[kudos_count]", rangeExpression(filters.kudosFrom, filters.kudosTo)) add("work_search[comments_count]", rangeExpression(filters.commentsFrom, filters.commentsTo)) add("work_search[bookmarks_count]", rangeExpression(filters.bookmarksFrom, filters.bookmarksTo)) add("work_search[date_from]", filters.dateFrom?.toString()) add("work_search[date_to]", filters.dateTo?.toString()) add("work_search[revised_at]", filters.updated.ao3Value) add("work_search[language_id]", filters.language.code) add("work_search[sort_column]", filters.sort.sortColumn) // iOS: never carry Ascending back onto Best Match (worst match first). if (filters.sort.sortColumn != null) { add("work_search[sort_direction]", filters.sortDirection.ao3Value) } builder.addQueryParameter("page", page.coerceAtLeast(1).toString())</code> | Tag and search endpoints share the emitter. ## SearchPanelRulesTests

| Test — guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `retryKeepsTheReaderOnTheirPage`<br>Retry stays on the requested/current result page; initial failure starts at page 1. | `KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:10` <code>#expect(SearchRetry.page(requested: 5, current: 4, hasResults: true) == 5)</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:11` <code>#expect(SearchRetry.page(requested: nil, current: 4, hasResults: true) == 4)</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:13` <code>#expect(SearchRetry.page(requested: 5, current: 1, hasResults: false) == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchLocalMatches.kt:161–164` <code>fun searchRetryPage(requested: Int?, current: Int, hasResults: Boolean): Int? { if (!hasResults) return null return requested ?: current }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchViewModel.kt:185–195` <code>fun retry() { val error = _state.value as? SearchUiState.Error ?: return val page = searchRetryPage(requestedPage, error.page, error.stale != null) if (page == null) { runSearch(1, lastFilters) } else if (error.stale != null) { _state.value = error.stale launchSearch(page, lastFilters, keepResults = true) } else { launchSearch(page, lastFilters, keepResults = false) }</code> | Matches requested=5/current=4/results and no-results cases. Android test: SearchLocalMatchesTest.retryPageFollowsResultsOnScreen |
| `aClosedPanelPutsBackTheSearchOnScreenAndNothingInBrowse`<br>Closing a panel restores loaded or in-flight filters while preserving typed query, unless idle. | `KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:29` <code>#expect(SearchPanelClose.restored(live: edited, loaded: abandoned, requested: nil, isIdle: false) == back)</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:30` <code>#expect(SearchPanelClose.restored(live: edited, loaded: nil, requested: abandoned, isIdle: false) == back)</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:32` <code>#expect(SearchPanelClose.restored(live: edited, loaded: nil, requested: abandoned, isIdle: true) == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:33` <code>#expect(SearchPanelClose.restored(live: edited, loaded: nil, requested: nil, isIdle: false) == nil)</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchViewModel.kt:139–142` <code>fun discardFilterEdits() { if (_state.value !is SearchUiState.Results) return _filters.value = lastFilters.copy(query = _filters.value.query) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchViewModel.kt:337–343` <code>private fun launchSearch(page: Int, searchFilters: AO3SearchFilters, keepResults: Boolean) { lastFilters = searchFilters requestedPage = page val stale = if (keepResults) _state.value as? SearchUiState.Results else null if (stale == null) { _state.value = SearchUiState.Loading _isPaging.value = false</code> | Only Results restores filters. During the first Loading state dismiss keeps unapplied edits; a later page tap sees filters != lastFilters and starts the wrong search. Android test: No exact Android test located. |
| `filterBadgeMatchesTheHeroFiltersCell`<br>The filter badge counts the hero's filter chips excluding subject and sort. | `KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:47` <code>#expect(SearchFilterBadge.count(for: filters) == heroCell)</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:48` <code>#expect(SearchFilterBadge.count(for: filters) == 2)</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:49` <code>#expect(SearchFilterBadge.count(for: AO3SearchFilters()) == 0)</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchFilterHelpers.kt:131–135` <code>fun activeFilterCount(filters: AO3SearchFilters): Int { if (!filters.hasActiveFilters) return 0 val labels = summaryLabels(filters) val facets = if (filters.sort == AO3SearchSort.RELEVANCE) labels.size - 1 else labels.size return facets + if (filters.sortDirection != AO3SortDirection.DESCENDING) 1 else 0</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchFilterHelpers.kt:162–165` <code>addTagField(SummaryLabel.fandomIcon, filters.fandom, filters.excludedFandoms) addTagField(SummaryLabel.characterIcon, filters.characters, filters.excludedCharacters) addTagField(SummaryLabel.relationshipIcon, filters.relationships, filters.excludedRelationships) addTagField(SummaryLabel.freeformIcon, filters.additionalTags, filters.excludedAdditionalTags)</code> | It counts the subject fandom too (3 rather than the test's 2), counts non-default sort and direction, and can disagree with the hero. No wrong AO3 parameter by this badge alone. Android test: SearchFilterHelpersTest.activeFilterCountReportsWhatTheUserChanged asserts the older counting rule. |
| `savedSearchNameIsTheSubject`<br>Saved-search naming follows the subject, not the first of several fandoms. | `KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:58` <code>#expect(SearchView.defaultSavedSearchName(for: twoFields) == "Saved Search")</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:60` <code>#expect(SearchView.defaultSavedSearchName(for: twoFields) == "enemies to lovers")</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:64` <code>#expect(SearchView.defaultSavedSearchName(for: oneCharacter) == "Character C")</code><br>`KudosTests/SearchPanelRulesTests.swift:69` <code>#expect(SearchView.defaultSavedSearchName(for: oneFandomAndQuery) == "Fandom A")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchFilterHelpers.kt:378–381` <code>fun defaultSavedSearchName(filters: AO3SearchFilters): String { val subject = filters.searchSubject().text return if (subject == SEARCH_RESULTS_FALLBACK) "Saved Search" else subject }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchLocalMatches.kt:117–133` <code>const val SEARCH_RESULTS_FALLBACK = "Search Results" fun AO3SearchFilters.searchSubject(): SearchSubject { val fields = listOf( warnings.singleOrNull()?.title.orEmpty() to SearchSubjectField.WARNING, fandom to SearchSubjectField.FANDOM, characters to SearchSubjectField.CHARACTER, relationships to SearchSubjectField.RELATIONSHIP, additionalTags to SearchSubjectField.FREEFORM ) val filled = fields.filter { it.first.isNotBlank() } if (filled.size == 1) { val names = AO3SearchFilters.commaSeparatedValues(filled.first().first) if (names.size == 1) return SearchSubject(names.first(), filled.first().second) } val text = query.trim() return SearchSubject(text.ifEmpty { SEARCH_RESULTS_FALLBACK }, null)</code> | One subject wins; multi-field fallback uses query or Saved Search. Android test: SearchFilterHelpersTest.defaultSavedSearchNameUsesTheSearchSubject |

## AO3URLResolverTests

| Test — guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `nilInputReturnsNil`<br>Null input resolves nowhere. | `KudosTests/AO3URLResolverTests.swift:10` <code>#expect(AO3URLResolver.resolve(nil) == nil)</code> | **not applicable** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3URLResolver.kt:19–25` <code>fun resolve( href: String, baseUrl: String = "https://archiveofourown.org/", allowExternalHost: Boolean = false ): String? { val trimmed = href.trim() if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return null</code> | The Kotlin API requires non-null String; there is no nullable-href resolver call to dispatch. Android test: No nullable-input Android test. |
| `emptyStringReturnsNil`<br>Empty href resolves nowhere. | `KudosTests/AO3URLResolverTests.swift:14` <code>#expect(AO3URLResolver.resolve("") == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3URLResolver.kt:24–30` <code>val trimmed = href.trim() if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return null if (trimmed.startsWith("javascript:", ignoreCase = true) &#124;&#124; trimmed.startsWith("data:", ignoreCase = true) &#124;&#124; trimmed.startsWith("mailto:", ignoreCase = true) ) { return null</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3URLResolver.kt:33–62` <code>val absolute = try { when { trimmed.startsWith("//") -&gt; "https:$trimmed" trimmed.startsWith("http://", ignoreCase = true) &#124;&#124; trimmed.startsWith("https://", ignoreCase = true) -&gt; trimmed else -&gt; URI(baseUrl).resolve(trimmed).toString() } } catch (_: Exception) { return null } val uri = try { URI(absolute) } catch (_: Exception) { return null } val scheme = uri.scheme?.lowercase() ?: return null if (scheme != "http" &amp;&amp; scheme != "https") return null val host = uri.host?.lowercase() ?: return null if (!allowExternalHost &amp;&amp; host !in trustedHosts) return null // Force https for AO3 hosts. val https = if (host in trustedHosts &amp;&amp; scheme == "http") { URI("https", uri.userInfo, host, uri.port, uri.path, uri.query, uri.fragment).toString() } else { absolute } return https</code> | The URI resolver and scheme/host checks keep this case. Android test: AO3URLResolverTest.resolvesRelativeWorkHref (partial) |

## AO3WriteActionsTests

| Test — guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `parsesCSRFTokenFromMetaTag`<br>A csrf-token meta preserves its exact token bytes. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteActionsTests.swift:16` <code>#expect(AO3Client.parseCSRFToken(from: html) == "aB3/dEf+gh==")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3WriteFormParser.kt:26–40` <code>fun parseAuthenticityToken(html: String, formSelector: String? = null, metaOnly: Boolean = false): String? { val document = Jsoup.parse(html) // iOS fetchCSRFPage uses the page's csrf-token meta, not another row's input. if (metaOnly) return document.selectFirst("meta[name=csrf-token]")?.attr("content")?.trim() ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } if (formSelector != null) { document.selectFirst(formSelector)?.authenticityToken()?.let { return it } } document.selectFirst("input[name=authenticity_token]")?.attr("value")?.trim() ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?.let { return it } return document.selectFirst("meta[name=csrf-token]")?.attr("content")?.trim() ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }</code> | On the tested meta-only page the fallback returns aB3/dEf+gh==. This does not prove form scoping when other inputs exist. Android test: AO3WriteParserTest.parsesTokenFromMetaTag |
| `missingCSRFTokenReturnsNil`<br>No CSRF token yields no token. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteActionsTests.swift:20` <code>#expect(AO3Client.parseCSRFToken(from: "&lt;html&gt;&lt;head&gt;&lt;/head&gt;&lt;/html&gt;") == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3WriteFormParser.kt:26–40` <code>fun parseAuthenticityToken(html: String, formSelector: String? = null, metaOnly: Boolean = false): String? { val document = Jsoup.parse(html) // iOS fetchCSRFPage uses the page's csrf-token meta, not another row's input. if (metaOnly) return document.selectFirst("meta[name=csrf-token]")?.attr("content")?.trim() ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } if (formSelector != null) { document.selectFirst(formSelector)?.authenticityToken()?.let { return it } } document.selectFirst("input[name=authenticity_token]")?.attr("value")?.trim() ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?.let { return it } return document.selectFirst("meta[name=csrf-token]")?.attr("content")?.trim() ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }</code> | Both input and meta lookup return null. Android test: AO3WriteParserTest.missingTokenReturnsNull |
| `parsesSelectedDefaultPseud`<br>The selected pseud option supplies the default posting ID. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteActionsTests.swift:32` <code>#expect(AO3Client.parseDefaultPseudID(from: html) == "22")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3WriteFormParser.kt:43–48` <code>fun parseDefaultPseudId(html: String, field: String = "comment[pseud_id]"): String? { val document = Jsoup.parse(html) val select = document.selectFirst("select[name=\"$field\"]") ?: return null val selected = select.selectFirst("option[selected]")?.attr("value")?.trim() if (!selected.isNullOrEmpty()) return selected return select.selectFirst("option")?.attr("value")?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }</code> | Select option 22 wins. Android test: AO3WriteParserTest.parsesSelectedDefaultPseudAndBookmarkPseud |
| `pseudFallsBackToFirstOptionWhenNoneSelected`<br>Without selected markup the first pseud option is used. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteActionsTests.swift:41` <code>#expect(AO3Client.parseDefaultPseudID(from: html) == "7")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3WriteFormParser.kt:43–48` <code>fun parseDefaultPseudId(html: String, field: String = "comment[pseud_id]"): String? { val document = Jsoup.parse(html) val select = document.selectFirst("select[name=\"$field\"]") ?: return null val selected = select.selectFirst("option[selected]")?.attr("value")?.trim() if (!selected.isNullOrEmpty()) return selected return select.selectFirst("option")?.attr("value")?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }</code> | Fallback returns ID 7. Android test: No exact Android test located. |

| `formEncodingPercentEncodesKeysAndValues`<br>Form keys and token values use percent encoding, including spaces and slashes. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteActionsTests.swift:422–423` <code>#expect(string == "authenticity_token=a%20b%2Fc&amp;kudo%5Bcommentable_id%5D=12345&amp;kudo%5Bcommentable_type%5D=Work")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:423–451` <code>object AO3FormEncoding { fun encode(fields: List&lt;Pair&lt;String, String&gt;&gt;): String { return fields.joinToString("&amp;") { (key, value) -&gt; "${percentEncode(key)}=${percentEncode(value)}" } } private fun percentEncode(value: String): String { val bytes = value.encodeToByteArray() val builder = StringBuilder() for (byte in bytes) { val unsigned = byte.toInt() and 0xff val char = unsigned.toChar() if ( char in 'A'..'Z' &#124;&#124; char in 'a'..'z' &#124;&#124; char in '0'..'9' &#124;&#124; char == '-' &#124;&#124; char == '.' &#124;&#124; char == '_' &#124;&#124; char == '~' ) { builder.append(char) } else { builder.append('%') builder.append(unsigned.toString(16).uppercase().padStart(2, '0')) } } return builder.toString()</code> | UTF-8 bytes encode as uppercase %HH; spaces are %20. Android test: AO3WriteParserTest.formEncodingMatchesApplePercentEncoding |

## AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests

| Test — guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `anUnchangedGenerationStillDispatchesOnce`<br>An unchanged session dispatches exactly one explicit-cookie write without leaking an internal stamp. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:134–136` <code>#expect( request.value(forHTTPHeaderField: AO3Client.preparedWriteSessionHeader) == stamp )</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:145` <code>#expect(status == 201)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:146` <code>#expect(body == "fence-ok")</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:148` <code>#expect(hits.count == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:149` <code>#expect(hits.first?.handlesCookies == false)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:150` <code>#expect(hits.first?.preparedWriteSession == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:151` <code>#expect(hits.first?.cookie?.contains("_otwarchive_session=session-alice") == true)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:152` <code>#expect(auth.writeSessionStamp == stamp)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:47–66` <code>override suspend fun postAuthenticatedInSession( url: String, formFields: List&lt;Pair&lt;String, String&gt;&gt;, headers: Map&lt;String, String&gt;, generation: Int? ): AO3Result&lt;AO3HttpResponse&gt; { fun requireSession() { if (generation != sessionGeneration()) throw CancellationException() } requireSession() val authHeaders = when (val result = authRepository.authenticatedHeaders(url)) { is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; return result is AO3Result.Success -&gt; result.value } requireSession() val response = postClient.postFormChecked(url, formFields, headers + authHeaders, ::requireSession) if (generation == sessionGeneration() &amp;&amp; response is AO3Result.Failure &amp;&amp; response.error == AO3Error.AuthenticationRequired ) authRepository.sessionDidExpire(generation) return response</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:186–195` <code>val result = coordinator.coordinate { currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() beforeSend() performOnce(method, url, headers, formBody) } if (result is AO3Result.Success) return result val error = (result as AO3Result.Failure).error retryNumber += 1 if (!retryPolicy.shouldRetry(method, error, retryNumber)) return result</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RetryPolicy.kt:14–20` <code>fun shouldRetry( method: AO3HttpMethod, error: AO3Error, retryNumber: Int ): Boolean { if (method != AO3HttpMethod.GET) return false if (retryNumber &gt; config.maxRetries) return false</code> | Android binds headers/fence to its auth client instead of a URLRequest stamp; no internal stamp is sent. Its default OkHttp stack has no cookie jar and relays explicit cookies. Android test: AO3WorkSaveDispatchTest.realSharedClientSendsIosHeadersAndEncodedBodyOnceAndNeverRetries429Or503 |
| `aGenerationChangeDuringThePacingWaitDoesNotDispatch`<br>A generation change while waiting for pacing stops POST dispatch. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:178` <code>#expect(auth.sessionGeneration == prepared)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:180` <code>#expect(auth.sessionGeneration != prepared)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:183–185` <code>#expect(throws: CancellationError.self) { try await task.value }</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:186` <code>#expect(AO3WriteDispatchProbe.recorded().isEmpty)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:53–62` <code>fun requireSession() { if (generation != sessionGeneration()) throw CancellationException() } requireSession() val authHeaders = when (val result = authRepository.authenticatedHeaders(url)) { is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; return result is AO3Result.Success -&gt; result.value } requireSession() val response = postClient.postFormChecked(url, formFields, headers + authHeaders, ::requireSession)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:186–189` <code>val result = coordinator.coordinate { currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() beforeSend() performOnce(method, url, headers, formBody)</code> | beforeSend runs inside the coordinator after the wait. Android test: AO3UnsubscribeDispatchTest.sessionChangeInsideSharedPacingWaitPreventsPostDispatch |
| `aGenerationChangeAfterTheRequestWasBuiltDoesNotDispatch`<br>A change after preparing a write's session stops dispatch. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:198` <code>#expect(auth.sessionGeneration != prepared)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:206–208` <code>#expect(throws: CancellationError.self) { try await auth.submitWrite(request, using: client) }</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:209` <code>#expect(AO3WriteDispatchProbe.recorded().isEmpty)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:47–62` <code>override suspend fun postAuthenticatedInSession( url: String, formFields: List&lt;Pair&lt;String, String&gt;&gt;, headers: Map&lt;String, String&gt;, generation: Int? ): AO3Result&lt;AO3HttpResponse&gt; { fun requireSession() { if (generation != sessionGeneration()) throw CancellationException() } requireSession() val authHeaders = when (val result = authRepository.authenticatedHeaders(url)) { is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; return result is AO3Result.Success -&gt; result.value } requireSession() val response = postClient.postFormChecked(url, formFields, headers + authHeaders, ::requireSession)</code> | The captured generation is checked before collecting headers and at the send boundary. | `anotherAuthServiceAtTheSameGenerationDoesNotDispatch`<br>A different auth-service instance cannot authorize another instance's prepared Cookie request. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:221` <code>#expect(otherAuth.sessionGeneration == auth.sessionGeneration)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:229–231` <code>#expect(throws: CancellationError.self) { try await otherAuth.submitWrite(request, using: client) }</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:232` <code>#expect(AO3WriteDispatchProbe.recorded().isEmpty)</code> | **not applicable** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:38–45` <code>class DefaultAO3AuthenticatedClient( private val getClient: AO3Client, private val postClient: AO3FormPostClient, private val authRepository: AO3AuthRepository ) : AO3AuthenticatedClient { override val sessionChanges: kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow&lt;Int&gt; get() = authRepository.generation override fun username(): String? = authRepository.username() override fun sessionGeneration(): Int = authRepository.generation.value</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:53–62` <code>fun requireSession() { if (generation != sessionGeneration()) throw CancellationException() } requireSession() val authHeaders = when (val result = authRepository.authenticatedHeaders(url)) { is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; return result is AO3Result.Success -&gt; result.value } requireSession() val response = postClient.postFormChecked(url, formFields, headers + authHeaders, ::requireSession)</code> | Android has no transferable prepared URLRequest/stamp API. Android test: No equivalent prepared-request Android test. |
| `anUnstampedWriteDoesNotDispatch`<br>A raw unstamped URLRequest must fail before dispatch. | `KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:243–245` <code>#expect(throws: CancellationError.self) { try await auth.submitWrite(request, using: client) }</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3WriteDispatchFenceTests.swift:246` <code>#expect(AO3WriteDispatchProbe.recorded().isEmpty)</code> | **not applicable** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:84–102` <code>override suspend fun postAuthenticated( url: String, formFields: List&lt;Pair&lt;String, String&gt;&gt;, headers: Map&lt;String, String&gt; ): AO3Result&lt;AO3HttpResponse&gt; { // The session at the moment of the call. This used to copy the cookie and then wait // its turn in the request queue with no further check: a comment posted just before // signing out went out afterwards under the account that had been left, and a 401 for // it signed out whoever was signed in by then (audit A17-3). The fenced path checks // the session again after the wait, immediately before sending, and expires only its // own session. val generation = sessionGeneration() return try { postAuthenticatedInSession(url, formFields, headers, generation) } catch (cancelled: CancellationException) { // Our own fence, not the caller being cancelled: tell the caller plainly so its // screen does not wait for an answer that will not come. currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() AO3Result.Failure(AO3Error.AuthenticationRequired)</code> | No URLRequest/stamp feature exists. postAuthenticated always captures the bound client's generation and enters the checked path. This says nothing about deliberately bypassing that API with the generic transport. Android test: AO3UnsubscribeDispatchTest.aPlainAuthenticatedPostQueuedBeforeSignOutIsNeverSent covers the ordinary API. |

## AO3AuthorActionFencingTests

| Test — guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |

| `moderationFormStagesForTheContextThatRequestedIt`<br>A moderation form stages only for the requesting author/session and busy clears. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:60` <code>#expect(model.pendingModerationForm?.kind == .block)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:61` <code>#expect(model.actionMessage == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:62` <code>#expect(!model.isPerformingModeration)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/author/AuthorProfileScreen.kt:301–325` <code>fun moderation(step: suspend (io.github.cidy02.kudos.network.ao3.writes.AO3WriteRepository, Int) -&gt; Unit) { val writes = seriesWrites ?: return if (moderationBusy) return val expectedSession = sessionGeneration moderationBusy = true scope.launch { try { step(writes, expectedSession) } catch (cancelled: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) { // The session changed before anything was sent, or the screen went: nothing to say. currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() } finally { moderationBusy = false } } } fun beginModeration(action: io.github.cidy02.kudos.network.ao3.author.AO3AuthorWebAction) { val expectedRoute = route moderation { writes, session -&gt; val result = writes.loadAuthorModerationForm(action, expectedRoute.username, session) if (route != expectedRoute &#124;&#124; sessionGeneration != session) return@moderation when (result) { is AO3Result.Success -&gt; pendingModeration = result.value is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; profileMessage = result.error.displayMessage()</code> | The loaded form becomes pendingModeration only after the context check. Android test: AuthorModerationTest.theTapReadsThePageOnceAndChangesNothing (repository, not UI staging) |
| `staleGenerationSuppressesModerationFormStaging`<br>A replaced session cannot stage the old moderation form or error. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:85` <code>#expect(model.pendingModerationForm == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:86` <code>#expect(model.actionMessage == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:87` <code>#expect(!model.isPerformingModeration)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:69–81` <code>override suspend fun getAuthenticated(url: String): AO3Result&lt;AO3HttpResponse&gt; { val generation = sessionGeneration() val headers = when (val result = authRepository.authenticatedHeaders(url)) { is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; return result is AO3Result.Success -&gt; result.value } val response = getClient.get(url, headers) if (generation != sessionGeneration()) throw CancellationException() if (response is AO3Result.Failure &amp;&amp; response.error == AO3Error.AuthenticationRequired) { authRepository.sessionDidExpire(generation) } return response</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/author/AuthorProfileScreen.kt:318–325` <code>fun beginModeration(action: io.github.cidy02.kudos.network.ao3.author.AO3AuthorWebAction) { val expectedRoute = route moderation { writes, session -&gt; val result = writes.loadAuthorModerationForm(action, expectedRoute.username, session) if (route != expectedRoute &#124;&#124; sessionGeneration != session) return@moderation when (result) { is AO3Result.Success -&gt; pendingModeration = result.value is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; profileMessage = result.error.displayMessage()</code> | GET throws on generation mismatch; the screen also checks its context. Android test: AuthorModerationTest.anotherSessionASignedOutReaderOrAnotherSiteAsksNothing (entry fence) |
| `authenticationRequiredExpiresTheSessionThatWasCaptured`<br>An authentication refusal expires the captured session and stages no form. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:99` <code>#expect(!auth.isLoggedIn)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:100` <code>#expect(auth.sessionHealth == .expired)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:101` <code>#expect(model.pendingModerationForm == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:76–80` <code>val response = getClient.get(url, headers) if (generation != sessionGeneration()) throw CancellationException() if (response is AO3Result.Failure &amp;&amp; response.error == AO3Error.AuthenticationRequired) { authRepository.sessionDidExpire(generation) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/author/AuthorProfileScreen.kt:323–325` <code>when (result) { is AO3Result.Success -&gt; pendingModeration = result.value is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; profileMessage = result.error.displayMessage()</code> | Only successful form loads stage pendingModeration. Android test: No exact Android UI test located. |
| `staleAuthenticationRequiredCannotLogOutTheReplacementAccount`<br>A stale authentication refusal cannot sign out the replacement account. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:122` <code>#expect(auth.isLoggedIn)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthorActionFencingTests.swift:123` <code>#expect(auth.username == "bob")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/writes/AO3AuthenticatedClient.kt:76–80` <code>val response = getClient.get(url, headers) if (generation != sessionGeneration()) throw CancellationException() if (response is AO3Result.Failure &amp;&amp; response.error == AO3Error.AuthenticationRequired) { authRepository.sessionDidExpire(generation) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/auth/AO3AuthRepository.kt:301–305` <code>suspend fun sessionDidExpire(expectedGeneration: Int? = null) { sessionMutex.withLock { if (expectedGeneration != null &amp;&amp; expectedGeneration != sessionGeneration) return clearSessionLocked() mutableState.value = AO3AuthState.Expired()</code> | The generation check occurs before sessionDidExpire and expiry checks again under its lock. labels.firstOrNull { it.attr("for").endsWith("_tag_set_attributes_${type.wire}_tagnames") }?.let { countRange(it.text())?.let { range -&gt; type to range } } }.toMap() } val ranges = linkedMapOf&lt;SignUpPromptKind, IntRange&gt;() for (heading in form.select("fieldset &gt; h3.heading")) { val kind = SignUpPromptKind.entries.firstOrNull { heading.text().startsWith(it.wire.replaceFirstChar(Char::uppercase)) } ?: continue val range = countRange(heading.text()) if (range == null) ranges.remove(kind) else ranges[kind] = range } val token = AO3WriteFormParser().parseAuthenticityToken(html, metaOnly = true) ?: input("authenticity_token").takeIf(String::isNotEmpty) ?: error("Couldn't read AO3's sign-up form.") val pseud = input("challenge_signup[pseud_id]").ifEmpty { form.select("select").firstOrNull { it.attr("name") == "challenge_signup[pseud_id]" } ?.let { it.selectFirst("option[selected]") ?: it.selectFirst("option") }?.attr("value").orEmpty() } return AO3ChallengeSignUpForm(slug, action, token, input("_method").takeIf(String::isNotEmpty), Regex("/signups/([0-9]+)(?:/&#124;$)").find(java.net.URI(action).path)?.groupValues?.get(1)?.toIntOrNull(), pseud, prompts(SignUpPromptKind.Request), prompts(SignUpPromptKind.Offer), ranges[SignUpPromptKind.Request]?.let { SignUpLimits(it, ranges[SignUpPromptKind.Offer] ?: 0..0) }, tagLimits(SignUpPromptKind.Request), tagLimits(SignUpPromptKind.Offer), AO3WorkFormParser().servedControls(doc, form),</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/account/AO3ChallengeSignUp.kt:90–108` <code>/** iOS challengeSignUpParameters + nestedPromptParameters, without hidden-field replay. */ fun iosParameters(): List&lt;Pair&lt;String, String&gt;&gt; Try again.", and a second tap sent the same claim again; A37 F10). is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; if (response.error is AO3Error.Network) AO3Result.Failure(AO3Error.Validation(AO3CollectionFields.NO_ANSWER)) else response is AO3Result.Success -&gt; { val error = parser.writeErrorMessage(response.value.body) when { error != null -&gt; AO3Result.Failure(AO3Error.Validation(error)) parser.writeSuccessMessage(response.value.body) != null &#124;&#124; response.value.statusCode in 300..399 -&gt; AO3Result.Success(Unit) response.value.statusCode in 200..299 -&gt; AO3Result.Failure(AO3Error.Validation(AO3CollectionFields.UNCONFIRMED)) else -&gt; AO3Result.Failure(AO3Error.Validation(fallback))</code> | The tested 200 notice, 302, error, maintenance 200 and 500 all receive the iOS verdict. | `keysNormalizeWhitespaceButPreserveCase`<br>Duplicate identity normalizes whitespace, preserves case, and distinguishes parent/account. | `KudosTests/CommentSubmissionTests.swift:26` <code>#expect(key(body: "Great chapter!\n") == key(body: "Great chapter!"))</code><br>`KudosTests/CommentSubmissionTests.swift:27` <code>#expect(key(body: "great chapter!") != key(body: "Great chapter!"))</code><br>`KudosTests/CommentSubmissionTests.swift:28` <code>#expect(key(body: "Great chapter!", parent: 5) != key(body: "Great chapter!"))</code><br>`KudosTests/CommentSubmissionTests.swift:29` <code>#expect(key(identity: "a") != key(identity: "b"))</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/comments/CommentsViewModel.kt:202–203` <code>/** Tracks the hash of the last successfully or ambiguously submitted content to prevent double-posts. */ private var lastSubmittedContentHash: Int? = null</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/comments/CommentsViewModel.kt:757–763` <code>val edit = sent.edit val replyTo = sent.replyTo val contentHash = content.hashCode() if (contentHash == lastSubmittedContentHash) { _message.value = "You just posted this. Reload to see if it appeared." return</code> | The key is only raw content.hashCode(): whitespace variants bypass it; different parents share it; hash collisions can block different text. No normalized context/account key exists. Android test: No equivalent Android key test located. |

| `draftsPersistPerContextAndEmptySaveClears`<br>Unsent drafts persist per work/chapter/parent and blank save removes only that draft. | `KudosTests/CommentSubmissionTests.swift:542` <code>#expect(store.draft(for: topLevel) == "Half-typed thought…")</code><br>`KudosTests/CommentSubmissionTests.swift:543` <code>#expect(store.draft(for: reply) == "Reply in progress")</code><br>`KudosTests/CommentSubmissionTests.swift:547` <code>#expect(store.draft(for: reply).isEmpty)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/comments/CommentDraftStore.kt:20–38` <code>private fun keyFor(workId: Long, chapterId: Long?, parentId: Long?, username: String?): String { return "draft:${workId}:${chapterId ?: 0}:${parentId ?: 0}:${username ?: "guest"}" } suspend fun getDraft(workId: Long, chapterId: Long? = null, parentId: Long? = null, username: String? = null): String? { val key = stringPreferencesKey(keyFor(workId, chapterId, parentId, username)) return dataStore.data.map { it[key] }.first() } suspend fun saveDraft(content: String, workId: Long, chapterId: Long? = null, parentId: Long? = null, username: String? = null) { val key = stringPreferencesKey(keyFor(workId, chapterId, parentId, username)) dataStore.edit { prefs -&gt; if (content.isBlank()) prefs.remove(key) else prefs[key] = content } } suspend fun clearDraft(workId: Long, chapterId: Long? = null, parentId: Long? = null, username: String? = null) { saveDraft("", workId, chapterId, parentId, username)</code> | DataStore keys retain the complete context and content.isBlank removes the entry. ## DownloadOnSubscribeTests

| `offByDefaultQueuesNothing`<br>The default/off Download on Subscribe setting queues nothing. | `KudosTests/DownloadOnSubscribeTests.swift:26` <code>#expect(WorkDetailView.downloadItem(for: work(), downloadOnSubscribe: false) == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/core/model/SettingsModels.kt:75–76` <code>/** iOS "downloadOnSubscribe": subscribing to a Library work without a copy downloads it. */ val downloadOnSubscribe: Boolean = false,</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkDetailScreen.kt:539–543` <code>// iOS downloadIfSubscribedWithoutEPUB: only a work already in the Library, and // only with the setting on; preserveEpubForQueue skips one that has its file. if (result.value.kind == AO3WriteActionKind.Subscribe &amp;&amp; settings.app.downloadOnSubscribe) { state.local?.let(::preserveEpubForQueue) }</code> | Queueing is conditional on the explicit setting and a successful Subscribe outcome. Android test: No exact Android toggle test located. |
| `alreadyDownloadedQueuesNothing`<br>A subscribed local work with an EPUB queues no redundant download. | `KudosTests/DownloadOnSubscribeTests.swift:34` <code>#expect(item == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkDetailScreen.kt:447–455` <code>fun preserveEpubForQueue(work: SavedWork) { if (work.hasEpub) return val ao3Id = WorkTags.ao3WorkIdFromUrl(work.sourceUrl) ?: return downloadQueue.enqueueLocal( ao3WorkId = ao3Id, title = work.title, sourceUrl = work.sourceUrl, force = false )</code> | hasEpub returns before enqueueLocal. Android test: DownloadQueueTest.skipAlreadyDownloadedWhenDetectable covers the queue's additional skip. |
| `emptySourceURLQueuesNothing`<br>An empty source URL queues nothing. | `KudosTests/DownloadOnSubscribeTests.swift:46` <code>#expect(item == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkDetailScreen.kt:447–455` <code>fun preserveEpubForQueue(work: SavedWork) { if (work.hasEpub) return val ao3Id = WorkTags.ao3WorkIdFromUrl(work.sourceUrl) ?: return downloadQueue.enqueueLocal( ao3WorkId = ao3Id, title = work.title, sourceUrl = work.sourceUrl, force = false )</code> | No AO3 work ID can be parsed from an empty source, so it returns. preserveEpubForQueue skips one that has its file. if (result.value.kind == AO3WriteActionKind.Subscribe &amp;&amp; settings.app.downloadOnSubscribe) { state.local?.let(::preserveEpubForQueue) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkDetailScreen.kt:447–455` <code>fun preserveEpubForQueue(work: SavedWork) { if (work.hasEpub) return val ao3Id = WorkTags.ao3WorkIdFromUrl(work.sourceUrl) ?: return downloadQueue.enqueueLocal( ao3WorkId = ao3Id, title = work.title, sourceUrl = work.sourceUrl, force = false )</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/DownloadQueue.kt:104–118` <code>fun enqueueLocal( ao3WorkId: Long, title: String, sourceUrl: String, force: Boolean = true ) { enqueue( listOf( DownloadQueueItem( ao3WorkId = ao3WorkId, title = title, sourceUrl = sourceUrl, force = force ) )</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/DownloadQueue.kt:207–214` <code>val existing = WorkIdentityIndex.findExisting( candidateSourceUrl = source, byId = { workRepository.getWork(it) }, bySourceUrl = { workRepository.findBySourceUrl(it) } ) if (existing != null) { return workImporter.downloadExisting(existing) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkImporter.kt:165–178` <code>suspend fun downloadExisting(work: SavedWork): WorkImportResult { val workId = WorkTags.ao3WorkIdFromUrl(work.sourceUrl) ?: return WorkImportResult.Failure(work, AO3Error.Validation("No AO3 work id found for this work.")) return when (val download = downloader.download(workId)) { is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; ## Input evidence and completion boundary

The private-bookmark finding feeds the iOS test's actual fixture into the quoted Kotlin selector. `KudosTests/Fixtures/ao3_work_bookmarked_subscribed.html:81` contains <code>&lt;input name="bookmark[private]" type="hidden" value="0" autocomplete="off" /&gt;&lt;input type="checkbox" value="1" checked="checked" name="bookmark[private]" id="bookmark_private" /&gt;</code>. Android's own `android/app/src/test/resources/ao3/writes/work_bookmarked.html:19–20` instead has <code>&lt;input type="checkbox" name="bookmark[private]" value="1" checked="checked"&gt;\n&lt;input type="checkbox" name="bookmark[rec]" value="1" checked="checked"&gt;</code>, with no preceding hidden twins. Its positive prefill test therefore does not cover the iOS input that fails. No inference from copied fixture names or an asset's mere presence was used as proof.

Android is plainly right on structured search fields and facet IDs, stable set emission, rating OR syntax, numeric ranges, local calendar-day date emission, relevance sorting, requested positive pagination, collection default/reset scopes, error-before-success classification, and the checked collection/challenge session fences. Collection deletion takes its token from the destroy form, refuses a missing form, and sends one delete POST; work deletion already avoids extra public-page reads. Download-on-subscribe stays off by default and avoids a file that is already present; Recently Deleted queue matches are restored. Its shared write transport rejects retries for every non-GET (`AO3RetryPolicy.kt:19`: <code>if (method != AO3HttpMethod.GET) return false</code>), and `AO3Client.kt:186–189` runs the captured session's check inside the paced slot immediately before sending. An extra comment POST admitted by the screen is a second operation, not a transport retry; that distinction is why both the positive dispatch rows and the negative single-flight rows can be true.

No time cutoff: all seventeen files and all 213 tests were examined. The six unsure rows are the extreme-slider offset comparison, two unimplemented Unmark race comparisons, and three internal comment adopt/verification-target state comparisons. These boundaries remain explicit in their rows. No builds, test execution, branch switches, sign-in, AO3 contact, commits, pushes, source edits, or TASKS.md edits were performed. Only this report was written. Findings are static control-flow predictions, not live AO3 or rendered-UI reproduction.

## The missing ones worth fixing first

1. **Private bookmark can become public:** parse the checkbox rather than its preceding hidden zero; an unchanged edit can send `bookmark[private]=0` and expose a previously private bookmark (`readsExistingBookmarkFromWorkPageEditForm`).
2. **Comments can be posted twice:** add synchronous single-flight admission and shared, account/context/body-keyed unresolved records with authoritative verification; a rapid repeat or reopening after a lost answer currently permits another POST (the related CommentSubmissionTests rows).
3. **Posting pseud is lost or wrong:** retain hidden single-pseud IDs, prefer them over a select, and refuse a page with no authorized pseud before POST; normal single-pseud comments/replies currently omit `comment[pseud_id]` (AO3WriteActionsTests and AO3PostingPseudTests missing rows).
4. **Unmark falsely changes the remote list:** replace local-only hiding with the fenced GET → one `mark_as_read` patch-override POST and remove the row only on AO3's confirmation (`unmarkForLaterPatchesMarkAsReadOnce`, `anUnmarkWithoutAO3sNoticeIsNotASuccess`).
5. **Schedule fields are reposted without a timezone:** omit schedule parameters when AO3 provides no timezone; disabling their editors alone still sends the old wall-clock values (`missingTimeZoneMakesScheduleReadOnlyAndOmitsItFromSave`).
6. **A saved future language silently becomes Any:** use the preserved unknown language ID in the live query as well as re-export, or show that the search cannot be represented; currently `language_id` disappears and AO3 returns other works (`languageDecodesEveryShapeItHasEverBeenWrittenIn`).
7. **Closing filters during the initial load leaves unapplied edits live:** restore the requested filter snapshot as well as loaded results, so the next page does not start a different search (`aClosedPanelPutsBackTheSearchOnScreenAndNothingInBrowse`).
8. **Draft identity/cleanup is incomplete:** move launch-specific unnamed-session drafts into the verified account and clear only the successful submission's chapter variants; stranded drafts and already-posted variants can mislead the reader into posting again (the last two CommentSubmissionTests rows).
9. **WebView navigation loses frame/demo distinctions:** keep foreign embeds in their frame and prevent demo navigation from opening an external remote page; the in-view demo interceptor itself already blocks remote fetches (`anEmbedFromAnotherHostIsAFrameNotThePage`, `theDemoLoadsNothingRemote`).
10. **Resolver trust rules differ:** accept AO3 download subdomains and reject HTTP AO3 input consistently with iOS; the present resolver rejects a valid AO3 subdomain and upgrades a URL iOS refuses (`subdomainHostIsAllowedByDefault`, `cleartextHTTPAbsoluteAO3URLIsRejected`).

Lower priority: the badge overcounts; arrow long-press differs despite separate First/Last buttons; tapped tag links lose passthrough parameters; filtered tag listings omit view_adult. The three challenge checkbox rows have a literal duplicate-pair/first-value difference, **but Android keeps the effective tick and disabled omission** by replaying the browser's hidden-zero/checked-one order. No wrong Rails setting is demonstrated for those three, and they should not displace the harmful fixes above.

## Triage (Claude, 2026-10-15 13:00)

213 iOS tests read; most kept on Android. Checked against the code before acting:

- **#1, real (P1, privacy):** fixed in **3ff**. `AO3WriteFormParser.parseBookmarkState` read the
  hidden `bookmark[private]=0` AO3 writes before the checkbox, so a private bookmark read as
  public and any edit saved it public (rec the same). Now reads the checkbox. Test on iOS's own
  fixture (`AO3BookmarkFormParseTest`), failing without the fix.
- **#3, real:** fixed in **3ff**. `parseDefaultPseudId` read only a select; a single-pseud
  account's hidden input was missed, so its comments went without a pseud. iOS's rule (hidden
  first) ported with its two tests.
- **#4, real:** Marked for Later's Unmark only hides the row, while the page says it removes it
  on AO3. Brief **3fg**.
- **#2, #5, #6, #7, #8, #10:** brief **3fg**, each to be confirmed against the code first.
- **#9 and the lower-priority list:** later.
